- **committing-with-lplp-style**: Activates the lplp-pipbuck commit style for the current session. Commits after every completed task, amends the last commit if its message is an `ai:` auto-commit (`ai: updated prompt`, `ai: save decision …`, or `ai: save plan …`), writes messages via ai/git/pending-commit.md, never commits unrelated files. Use when the user opts in to this style at session start, or explicitly asks to enable it. ([luckydonald/userscripts](https://github.com/luckydonald/userscripts/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/committing-with-lplp-style.md))
